LIGGGHTS (Version LIGGGHTS-PUBLIC 3.8.0, compiled 2026-08-31-14:14:39 by sikka-mac, git commit ed9b0f0f6262ca47bfb780e26993bf569811ffcc)
### Avalanching experiment with mixture of different density particles (metallic and rocky) at 25% metallic by number

### Initialization
# Preliminaries
units           si
atom_style      hybrid granular dipole
atom_modify     sort 10000 0

# boundaries
boundary        f f f

newton          off
hard_particles  yes
communicate     single vel yes

# the product of the processors must equal P, the number of processors used when running the file: mpirun -np 4 ...
processors      3 2 1
# running on 36 processors on dustiest


### VARIABLES DEFINITION

# Dimensions of the box
variable        dom_length equal 3 # 3 meters
variable        dom_width  equal 1 # 1 meter
variable        dom_height equal 1.5 # 1.5 meters
variable        mid_wall   equal 1.5 # 1.5 meters

# Declare domain
region          domain block 0 ${mid_wall} 0 ${dom_width} 0 ${dom_height} units box
region          domain block 0 1.5 0 ${dom_width} 0 ${dom_height} units box
region          domain block 0 1.5 0 1 0 ${dom_height} units box
region          domain block 0 1.5 0 1 0 1.5 units box
# Five types of materials to be used - 1,2,3: particles, 4: floor, 5: walls
create_box      5 domain
Created orthogonal box = (0 0 0) to (1.5 1 1.5)
  3 by 2 by 1 MPI processor grid

# variable        dom_dia equal qrt(${dom_length}*${dom_length}+${dom_width}*${dom_width}+${dom_height}*${dom_height})

# Particle diameter range
variable        particle_dia equal 0.05 # 5 cm
variable        particle_radius equal ${particle_dia}/2
variable        particle_radius equal 0.05/2

# Neighbor skin distance (fixed for all simulations)
variable        skin_dist equal  0.15 #meters

################################
# Material properties
################################

# METALLIC REGOLITH (KAMACITE, Fe-Ni ALLOY)
variable        density_kamacite equal 7870 # kg/m3 # Tanton et al 2020
variable        youngsM_kamacite equal 150.96e7 # N/m^2 # Tang 2023
variable        poissons_kamacite equal 0.17 # Tang 2023
variable        mag_susc_kamacite equal 20.0 # Assumed

# METALLIC REGOLITH (TROILITE, FeS)
variable        density_troilite equal 4900 # kg/m3 # Tanton et al 2020
variable        youngsM_troilite equal 6.0e9 # N/m^2 # Tang 2023
variable        poissons_troilite equal 0.25 # Tang 2023
variable        mag_susc_troilite equal 5.0 # Assumed

# ROCKY ASTEROID REGOLITH (Frizzell et al 2025)
variable        density_rocky equal 2500 # kg/m3
variable        youngsM_rocky equal 5e6 # N/m^2
variable        poissons_rocky equal 0.20
variable        mag_susc_rocky equal 0.0 # NOT MAGNETIC

# Acrylic (Sikka et al 2026)
variable        density_acrylic equal 1250 # kg/m3
variable        youngsM_acrylic equal 3e7 # N/m^2
variable        poissons_acrylic equal 0.4
variable        mag_susc_acrylic equal 0.0 # NOT MAGNETIC

################################
# Choosing material for particles, walls and floor
# Change these variables to change material types
################################

# Particle material type 1
# Using Kamacite for high magnetic susceptibility case
variable        density_particle1 equal ${density_kamacite}
variable        density_particle1 equal 7870
variable        youngsM_particle1 equal ${youngsM_kamacite}
variable        youngsM_particle1 equal 1509600000
variable        poissons_particle1 equal ${poissons_kamacite}
variable        poissons_particle1 equal 0.17
variable        magnetic_susc_particle1 equal ${mag_susc_kamacite}
variable        magnetic_susc_particle1 equal 20

# Particle material type 2
# Using Troilite for mixture
variable        density_particle2 equal ${density_troilite}
variable        density_particle2 equal 4900
variable        youngsM_particle2 equal ${youngsM_troilite}
variable        youngsM_particle2 equal 6000000000
variable        poissons_particle2 equal ${poissons_troilite}
variable        poissons_particle2 equal 0.25
variable        magnetic_susc_particle2 equal ${mag_susc_troilite}
variable        magnetic_susc_particle2 equal 5

# Particle material type 3
# Using Rocky material for mixture
variable        density_particle3 equal ${density_rocky}
variable        density_particle3 equal 2500
variable        youngsM_particle3 equal ${youngsM_rocky}
variable        youngsM_particle3 equal 5000000
variable        poissons_particle3 equal ${poissons_rocky}
variable        poissons_particle3 equal 0.2
variable        magnetic_susc_particle3 equal ${mag_susc_rocky}
variable        magnetic_susc_particle3 equal 0

# Floor material type
# Same as particles type 1 - Kamacite
variable        density_floor equal ${density_kamacite}
variable        density_floor equal 7870
variable        youngsM_floor equal ${youngsM_kamacite}
variable        youngsM_floor equal 1509600000
variable        poissons_floor equal ${poissons_kamacite}
variable        poissons_floor equal 0.17
variable        magnetic_susc_floor equal ${mag_susc_kamacite}
variable        magnetic_susc_floor equal 20

# Wall material type
# Using Acrylic for walls
variable        density_wall equal ${density_acrylic}
variable        density_wall equal 1250
variable        youngsM_wall equal ${youngsM_acrylic}
variable        youngsM_wall equal 30000000
variable        poissons_wall equal ${poissons_acrylic}
variable        poissons_wall equal 0.4
variable        magnetic_susc_wall equal ${mag_susc_acrylic}
variable        magnetic_susc_wall equal 0
# ################################

# KEEPING FRICTION, RESTITUTION, COHESION CONSTANT FOR ALL SIMULATIONS
# BASED ON FRIZZELL ET AL 2025 and SIKKA ET AL 2026

# Friction Properties
variable        friction_coeff_11 equal 1.0 # metallic1-metallic1
variable        friction_coeff_12 equal 1.0 # metallic1-metallic2
variable        friction_coeff_13 equal 1.0 # metallic1-rocky
variable        friction_coeff_14 equal 1.0 # metallic1-floor
variable        friction_coeff_15 equal 0.1 # metallic1-wall

variable        friction_coeff_21 equal ${friction_coeff_12}
variable        friction_coeff_21 equal 1
variable        friction_coeff_22 equal 1.0 # metallic2-metallic2
variable        friction_coeff_23 equal 1.0 # metallic2-rocky
variable        friction_coeff_24 equal 1.0 # metallic2-floor
variable        friction_coeff_25 equal 0.1 # metallic2-wall

variable        friction_coeff_31 equal ${friction_coeff_13}
variable        friction_coeff_31 equal 1
variable        friction_coeff_32 equal ${friction_coeff_23}
variable        friction_coeff_32 equal 1
variable        friction_coeff_33 equal 1.0 # rocky-rocky
variable        friction_coeff_34 equal 1.0 # rocky-floor
variable        friction_coeff_35 equal 0.1 # rocky-wall

variable        friction_coeff_41 equal ${friction_coeff_14}
variable        friction_coeff_41 equal 1
variable        friction_coeff_42 equal ${friction_coeff_24}
variable        friction_coeff_42 equal 1
variable        friction_coeff_43 equal ${friction_coeff_34}
variable        friction_coeff_43 equal 1
variable        friction_coeff_44 equal 0.1 # floor-floor (doesn't interact)
variable        friction_coeff_45 equal 0.1 # floor-wall (doesn't interact)

variable        friction_coeff_51 equal ${friction_coeff_15}
variable        friction_coeff_51 equal 0.1
variable        friction_coeff_52 equal ${friction_coeff_25}
variable        friction_coeff_52 equal 0.1
variable        friction_coeff_53 equal ${friction_coeff_35}
variable        friction_coeff_53 equal 0.1
variable        friction_coeff_54 equal ${friction_coeff_45}
variable        friction_coeff_54 equal 0.1
variable        friction_coeff_55 equal 0.1 # wall-wall (doesn't interact)

# Rolling Friction Properties

# Rolling friction coefficient
variable        rolling_coeff_11 equal 0.7 # metallic1-metallic1
variable        rolling_coeff_12 equal 0.7 # metallic1-metallic2
variable        rolling_coeff_13 equal 0.7 # metallic1-rocky

variable        rolling_coeff_32 equal ${rolling_coeff_23}
variable        rolling_coeff_32 equal 0.7
variable        rolling_coeff_33 equal 0.7 # rocky-rocky
variable        rolling_coeff_34 equal 0.7 # rocky-floor
variable        rolling_coeff_35 equal 0.01 # rocky-wall

variable        rolling_coeff_41 equal ${rolling_coeff_14}
variable        rolling_coeff_41 equal 0.7
variable        rolling_coeff_42 equal ${rolling_coeff_24}
variable        rolling_coeff_42 equal 0.7
variable        rolling_coeff_43 equal ${rolling_coeff_34}
variable        rolling_coeff_43 equal 0.7
variable        rolling_coeff_44 equal 0.01 # floor-floor (doesn't interact)
variable        rolling_coeff_45 equal 0.01 # floor-wall (doesn't interact)

variable        rolling_coeff_51 equal ${rolling_coeff_15}
variable        rolling_coeff_51 equal 0.01
variable        rolling_coeff_52 equal ${rolling_coeff_25}
variable        rolling_coeff_52 equal 0.01
variable        rolling_coeff_53 equal ${rolling_coeff_35}
variable        rolling_coeff_53 equal 0.01
variable        rolling_coeff_54 equal ${rolling_coeff_45}
variable        rolling_coeff_54 equal 0.01
variable        rolling_coeff_55 equal 0.01 # wall-wall (doesn't interact)

# Rolling Damp
variable        rolling_damp_11 equal 2.0 # metallic1-metallic1
variable        rolling_damp_12 equal 2.0 # metallic1-metallic2
variable        rolling_damp_13 equal 2.0 # metallic1-rocky
variable        rolling_damp_14 equal 2.0 # metallic1-floor
variable        rolling_damp_15 equal 0.01 # metallic1-wall

variable        rolling_damp_21 equal ${rolling_damp_12}
variable        rolling_damp_21 equal 2
variable        rolling_damp_22 equal 2.0 # metallic2-metallic2
variable        rolling_damp_23 equal 2.0 # metallic2-rocky

variable        rolling_damp_45 equal 0.01 # floor-wall (doesn't interact)

variable        rolling_damp_51 equal ${rolling_damp_15}
variable        rolling_damp_51 equal 0.01
variable        rolling_damp_52 equal ${rolling_damp_25}

# Coefficient of Restitution
variable        coeff_rest_11 equal 0.85 # metallic1-metallic1
variable        coeff_rest_12 equal 0.85  # metallic1-metallic2
variable        coeff_rest_13 equal 0.85  # metallic1-rocky
variable        coeff_rest_14 equal 0.85  # metallic1-floor
variable        coeff_rest_15 equal 0.4  # metallic1-wall

variable        coeff_rest_21 equal ${coeff_rest_12}
variable        coeff_rest_21 equal 0.85
variable        coeff_rest_22 equal 0.85 # metallic2-metallic2
variable        coeff_rest_23 equal 0.85 # metallic2-rocky

variable        coeff_rest_53 equal 0.4

# Cohesion Energy Density (J/m3)
variable        cohesion_energy equal 1e3
variable        cohesion_energy_11 equal ${cohesion_energy} # metallic1-metallic1
variable        cohesion_energy_11 equal 1000 
variable        cohesion_energy_12 equal ${cohesion_energy} # metallic1-metallic2
variable        cohesion_energy_12 equal 1000

variable        cohesion_energy_21 equal ${cohesion_energy_12}

variable        earth_g equal 9.82 #m/s^2

# Time steps
variable        dt equal 2e-6 # s every 2 microseconds
variable        dumptime equal 1e-3 # s every millisecond
variable        dumpstep equal ${dumptime}/${dt}
variable        dumpstep equal 0.001/${dt}
variable        dumpstep equal 0.001/2e-06
variable        thermostep equal ${dumpstep}
variable        thermostep equal 500
variable        checktimestep equal 1e-3/${dt}
variable        checktimestep equal 1e-3/2e-06
variable        neighstep equal 1e-2/${dt} # create a new neigh list
variable        neighstep equal 1e-2/2e-06 

# MAGNETIC TIME NEEDED IF MAGNETIC FORCES ARE USED

# Variables for run times
variable        half_second_steps equal 0.5/${dt} # number of steps for 0.5 seconds time
variable        half_second_steps equal 0.5/2e-06 
variable        one_second_steps equal 1/${dt} # number of steps for 1.0 seconds time
variable        one_second_steps equal 1/2e-06 
variable        0_1_second_steps equal 0.1/${dt}
variable        0_1_second_steps equal 0.1/2e-06

## DEFINITING SIM PROPERTIES
# Neighbor skin style - atom pairs within a neighbor cutoff distance = force cutoff + skin distance, styele = bin
neighbor        ${skin_dist} bin
neighbor        0.15 bin
# neigh_modify delay 0, this is the default, rebuild neighbor list every timestep
neigh_modify    delay 0 check yes

fix             m1 all property/global youngsModulus peratomtype ${youngsM_particle1} ${youngsM_particle2} ${youngsM_particle3} ${youngsM_floor} ${youngsM_wall}
fix             m1 all property/global youngsModulus peratomtype 1509600000 ${youngsM_particle2} ${youngsM_particle3} ${youngsM_floor} ${youngsM_wall}
fix             m1 all property/global youngsModulus peratomtype 1509600000 6000000000 ${youngsM_particle3} ${youngsM_floor} ${youngsM_wall}
fix             m1 all property/global youngsModulus peratomtype 1509600000 6000000000 5000000 ${youngsM_floor} ${youngsM_wall}
fix             m1 all property/global youngsModulus peratomtype 1509600000 6000000000 5000000 1509600000 ${youngsM_wall}
fix             m1 all property/global youngsModulus peratomtype 1509600000 6000000000 5000000 1509600000 30000000
fix             m2 all property/global poissonsRatio peratomtype ${poissons_particle1} ${poissons_particle2} ${poissons_particle3} ${poissons_floor} ${poissons_wall}
fix             m2 all property/global poissonsRatio peratomtype 0.17 ${poissons_particle2} ${poissons_particle3} ${poissons_floor} ${poissons_wall}
fix             m2 all property/global poissonsRatio peratomtype 0.17 0.25 ${poissons_particle3} ${poissons_floor} ${poissons_wall}
fix             m2 all property/global poissonsRatio peratomtype 0.17 0.25 0.2 ${poissons_floor} ${poissons_wall}
fix             m2 all property/global poissonsRatio peratomtype 0.17 0.25 0.2 0.17 ${poissons_wall}
fix             m2 all property/global poissonsRatio peratomtype 0.17 0.25 0.2 0.17 0.4
fix             m3 all property/global coefficientRestitution peratomtypepair 5 ${coeff_rest_11} ${coeff_rest_12} ${coeff_rest_13} ${coeff_rest_14} ${coeff_rest_15}                                         ${coeff_rest_21} ${coeff_rest_22} ${coeff_rest_23} ${coeff_rest_24} ${coeff_rest_25} ${coeff_rest_31} ${coeff_rest_32}                                         ${coeff_rest_33} ${coeff_rest_34} ${coeff_rest_35} ${coeff_rest_41} ${coeff_rest_42} ${coeff_rest_43} ${coeff_rest_44} ${coeff_rest_45}                                         ${coeff_rest_51} ${coeff_rest_52} ${coeff_rest_53} ${coeff_rest_54} ${coeff_rest_55}
